`_fast_len(m)`:
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

`_convolve_fft(a,b)`:
    L=_fast_len(len(a)+len(b)-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)
    return c[:len(a)+len(b)-1]
For real, ensure float64.

`_score_internal(a)`:
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return inf
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 1e-15: return inf
    if n < 500? We can use direct? FFT okay.
    a=a/s
    c=_convolve_fft(a,a)
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    if maxc <0: maxc=0.0
    return float(2.0*n*maxc)  # since sum=1
If we don't want to modify a copy heavily, okay.

`_score_raw(a)` maybe original sum: 2n*max_c/s^2. For final best from normalized sum1 same.

`_make_anti_t(n,rng)`:
    if n %2 !=0: n-=1? We'll require even.
    m=n//2
    base_len=(m+1)//2
    base=rng.choice([-1.0,1.0], size=base_len)
    u=np.empty(m)
    u[:base_len]=base
    u[base_len:]=base[:m-base_len][::-1]
    t=np.empty(n)
    t[:m]=u
    t[m:]=-u[::-1]
    return t

`_initial_P(x,t)`:
    n=len(t); ones=np.ones(n)
    A=np.arange(1,n)+np.arange(n-1,0,-1)
    C=_convolve_fft(t,t)
    D=_convolve_fft(t,ones)
    P=A + 2*x*D + x*x*C
    return P

Check D length: len(t)+n-1=2n-1 yes. For A length 2n-1.

Mirror descent details with correct gradient:
For weights w_k (sum1) over convolution max, gradient of J=∑w_k c_k wrt a_j is 2 T1_j = 2∑_{r=0}^{n-1} w_{j+r} a_r. We'll compute `grad = conv(w, a_rev)[n-1:n-1+n]` (no factor 2). For lse weights w sum1.
Update in log space: d = grad - grad.mean(); scale = max(np.std(d), 1e-12); u = d/scale. a_new = a * exp(-step*u); normalize. Need if some a become tiny. We can also use `np.log(a)` to avoid underflow? Step small and exp. Use `a = np.where(a>0, a, tiny)`. Initialize positive.
Step size: We can try step in [0.01,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5]. For each, candidate. But computing candidate scores for multiple steps expensive. We can greedily: step=0.1; if no improve for several, reduce; if improve increase. Use internal score.

But mirror descent might not escape from stationary uniform? With eps=0.2, weights over many diagonals, grad not uniform? At uniform a=1/n, grad_j =∑_{r} w_{j+r}/n. For central w symmetric, grad at center positions may differ? weights symmetric under k ->2n-2-k. For position j, sum over r=0..n-1 w_{j+r}. This is sliding window sum of w length n. For symmetric triangular w_max increasing center, sliding window sum is highest at center j=(n?); at edges lower. So grad nonuniform. Good. It will reduce center. May produce U-shaped. We can run.

`_continuous_local_search(a0, budget_s, eps_fracs, steps, rng)`:
   a=normalize positive (add tiny noise if zeros to allow increase)
   current score via _score_internal
   best tracked
   best_epoch = 0
   while time:
       c = conv_self(a) (we can get via convolve_fft)
       maxc = c.max()
       # maybe if maxc small? no
       for eps_frac in cycle [0.3,0.15,0.05,0.02,0.1,0.05]:
           compute w
           grad = convolution(w, a_rev)
           d=grad - grad.mean(); if std<1e-15: break/shift
           u=d/std
           # propose a_new with step
           candidates: step=0.2, 0.5, 0.1 maybe
           a_new = a*np.exp(-step*u); normalize; score; accept best
           if no improvement: step reduce, random perturb exp(noise)
       # occasionally random block perturbation
   return best

Let's implement continuous local more concretely.

`_continuous_local_search(a0, budget_s, rng, max_step=0.2)`:
   a = _normalize(a0).astype(float)
   n=a.size
   if n<10: return a
   # ensure positive
   a = np.clip(a, 1e-12, None); a/=a.sum()
   cur = _score_internal(a)
   best = a.copy(); best_score=cur
   step=0.1
   stagn=0
   while time.monotonic() < t0+budget_s and stagn < 5000:
       c = _conv_self(a)
       maxc = float(np.max(c))
       if not finite: break
       eps = 0.1*maxc  # maybe cycle
       z = (c - maxc)/eps
       zmax = float(np.max(z))
       w = np.exp(z - zmax)
       s = w.sum()
       if s ==0 or not finite: w = c==maxc? else
       w /= s
       # gradient
       conv = _convolve_fft(w, a[::-1]) # length 3n-2
       grad = conv[n-1:n-1+n]
       d = grad - np.mean(grad)
       scale = float(np.std(d))
       if scale < 1e-14:
           a = a * np.exp(0.05*(rng.normal(0,1,n)-...)); normalize
           continue
       u = d/scale
       # propose
       a_new = a * np.exp(-step*u)
       a_new = np.clip(a_new, 1e-300, None)
       sn = a_new.sum()
       if sn <=0: a_new = a
       a_new /= sn
       new = _score_internal(a_new)
       if new < cur:
           a = a_new; cur = new
           best = a.copy(); best_score = new
           stagn=0
           step = min(step*1.05, 0.5)
       else:
           # try smaller step
           a_small = a * np.exp(-0.25*step*u)
           a_small = _normalize(a_small)
           new2 = _score_internal(a_small)
           if new2 < cur:
              a=a_small; cur=new2; best=...
           else:
              # random small perturbation
              a += 0.01*rng.normal(0,1,n)
              a = _normalize(a)
              cur = _score_internal(a)
              if cur < best_score: best=...
              step *= 0.7
              if step < 0.01: step=0.05
           stagn +=1
       # periodically add tiny epsilon to zeros
       if len(a)>0 and np.min(a) < 1e-9:
           a += 1e-9
           a /= a.sum()
   return best

`_fourier_search` details:
```
def _fourier_search(n, budget_s, rng):
    D = 10  # modes terms count
    freqs = list(range(1, D//2+1))
    basis=[]
    for f in freqs:
       basis.append(np.sin(2π f i/n))
       basis.append(np.cos(...))
    B = np.column_stack([np.ones(n)] + basis) shape n x (1+D)
    bounds_lower = np.array([-1]* (1+D)); upper ... base lower 0.2 upper 3
    def eval_params(p): raw = B @ p; a=clip; norm; score; return score,a
    best_p=None; best_score=inf; best_a=None
    starts: zero; base=1 only; for each coord ±0.5; 20 random
    for each start, allocate maybe 0.2s; coordinate random walk.
```
No closures: define top-level `_eval_fourier(p, basis, bounds)` maybe. But basis passed. Use helper.

Time: Construct B for n=4096,D=10 -> 4096x11 ~45k, fine. Each eval: matvec n*11 ~45k, normalize, FFT score. 1000 evals per n ~45M ops, okay.

Hill climb:
```
p = start.copy(); cur,_=eval
while t<end:
   idx = rng.integers(0, len(p))
   if idx==0: delta = step * rng.normal(0,1) * max(0.1, p[0]*0.2)
   else: delta = step * rng.normal(0,1) * 0.3
   p_new[idx]=clip(p[idx]+delta,bounds)
   score,a=eval
   if score<cur: accept; step*=1.05; stall=0; update best
   else: step*=0.8; stall++; if step<1e-3 or stall>500: break
   also occasionally move multiple coords: idx choose set of 2.
```
At end consider best_a.

`_update_state(state,pool,seq)`:
   if seq is None return
   a = np.asarray(seq,float); if size==0 return
   a = clip; if sum<1e-9 return

def _continuous_local_search(a0, budget_s, rng):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return a
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-10)
    a /= a.sum()
    cur = _score_internal(a)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=cur
    step=0.05
    eps_idx=0
    stall=0
    it=0
    t0=time.monotonic(); end=t0+budget_s
    while time.monotonic()<end and stall<8000 and step>0.005:
        c = _conv_self(a)
        maxc=float(np.max(c))
        if not finite: break
        frac = [0.25,0.12,0.05,0.2,0.08][eps_idx % 5]
        if stall>1000: frac=0.3
        w = _smooth_weights(c, frac)
        g = _conv_weight_grad(w,a)
        d = g - np.mean(g)
        sd = float(np.std(d))
        if sd < 1e-14:
            a = a + (1.0/n)*rng.normal(0,0.05,n)
            a=_normalize(a); continue
        u=d/sd
        # try multiplier step and maybe 0.5step
        tried=0
        accepted=False
        for mult in (1.0,0.5,0.25):
            a_new = a * np.exp(-step*mult*u)
            a_new = np.maximum(a_new,1e-300); sn=a_new.sum(); if sn>0: a_new/=sn
            snew = _score_internal(a_new)
            if snew < cur:
                a=a_new; cur=snew; accepted=True; break
        if accepted:
            best=a.copy(); best_score=cur; stall=0; step=min(step*1.1,0.3)
        else:
            # random small block perturbation
            a = _block_perturb(a, rng, 3, 0.2)
            a = _normalize(a)
            cur = _score_internal(a)
            if cur < best_score: best=a.copy(); best_score=cur
            step = max(step*0.8,0.01)
            stall +=1
            if stall%500==0: eps_idx +=1; step=0.1
    return best
```

`_block_perturb`: 
```
def _block_perturb(a,rng,num_blocks=3,sigma=0.2):
   out=a.copy(); n=out.size
   for _ in range(num_blocks):
      w=int(rng.integers(1, max(2,n//10)))
      s=int(rng.integers(0,max(1,n-w)))
      out[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma))
   out=np.clip(out,0,1000)
   return out
```

def _normalize_sequence(seq):
   arr=np.asarray(seq, dtype=float)
   if arr.ndim!=1 or arr.size==0: return None
   arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
   s=arr.sum()
   if not finite or s<1e-9: return None
   arr=arr/s
   return arr

def _consider(state, pool, seq):
   arr=_normalize_sequence(seq)
   if arr is None: return
   score=_score_internal(arr)
   if score < state['score'] - 1e-12:
       state['seq']=arr.copy(); state['score']=score
       print(f"improved to {score:.6f} len={arr.size} time={time.monotonic()-state['t0']:.1f}")
   if pool is not None:
       pool.append((score, arr.copy()))
       if len(pool)>16:
           # remove worst: sort? For small list, simple
           worst = max(range(len(pool)), key=lambda idx: pool[idx][0])  # lambda not allowed!
```

def _resize_sequence(x, n):
    arr=np.asarray(x,float); arr=clip; if len==0 return None
    if len==n: return arr/arr.sum()
    if len < n:
        # pad by repeating? Use np.interp to n: maybe produce smooth
        old_len=len(arr)
        old=np.arange(old_len)
        new=np.linspace(0, old_len-1, n)
        arr=np.interp(new, old, arr)
    else:
        # downsample by average of ceil segments
        chunks=npy.array_split(np.arange(len(arr)), n)
        arr=np.array([arr[i].mean() for i in chunks]) # list comprehension maybe okay? Not lambda? list comp okay? It is like lambda? It's fine but rule no lambda. List comp not lambda. But top-level function can use list comprehension.
    arr=clip; s=arr.sum(); if s<=0: arr=ones; return arr/s

Detailed code outline:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _fast_len(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()

def _convolve_fft(x,y):
    x=np.asarray(x,float); y=np.asarray(y,float)
    n=len(x)+len(y)-1
    L=_fast_len(n)
    fx=np.fft.rfft(x,L); fy=np.fft.rfft(y,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fx*fy,L)
    return out[:n]

def _conv_self(x):
    return _convolve_fft(x,x)

def _normalize_array(x):
    a=np.asarray(x,float)
    if a.ndim !=1 or a.size==0: return None
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return None
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-12: return None
    a=a/s
    return a

def _score_internal(a):
    a=_normalize_array(a)
    if a is None: return np.inf
    n=a.size
    c=_conv_self(a)
    mc=float(np.max(c))
    if mc < 0: mc=0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mc): return np.inf
    return float(2.0*n*mc)
...
```

`_smooth_weights(c, frac)`:
```
def _smooth_weights(c, frac):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    if maxc <= 0 or not np.isfinite(maxc): return np.ones(c.size)/c.size
    eps=frac*maxc
    z=(c-maxc)/eps
    zm=float(np.max(z))
    w=np.exp(z-zm)
    s=float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0:
       mask = c >= maxc - 1e-12*maxc
       cnt=int(np.sum(mask))
       if cnt==0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w=np.zeros(c.size); w[idx]=1
       else:
          w=np.zeros(c.size); w[mask]=1/cnt
       return w
    return w/s
```

`_grad_weighted(w,a)`:
```
def _grad_weighted(w,a):
    n=len(a)
    L=_fast_len(3*n-2)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],L)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(fw*fa,L)
    return conv[n-1:n-1+n]
```
Return float.

def _eval_fourier(p, basis):
    raw = basis @ p  # basis n x D
    raw = np.clip(raw,0,1000)
    a = raw / max(raw.sum(),1e-300) if raw.sum()>0 else None
    if a is None or np.max(a)==0: return np.inf, None
    return _score_internal(a), a
```

Let's refine `_fourier_one` step to be effective:
```
def _fourier_one(n, budget_s, rng, state, pool):
    t0=time.monotonic(); end=t0+budget_s
    if n < 64: return
    D = 10
    freqs = list(range(1, D//2 + 1))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=float)
    cols=[np.ones(n)]
    for f in freqs:
       ang=2*np.pi*f*i/n
       cols.append(np.sin(ang)); cols.append(np.cos(ang))
    B=np.column_stack(cols)
    Dtot=B.shape[1]
    lower=np.full(Dtot,-1.0); lower[0]=0.05
    upper=np.full(Dtot,1.0); upper[0]=3.0
    best_p=np.zeros(Dtot); best_p[0]=1.0
    cur_score, best_a = _eval_fourier(best_p,B)
    if not fin: cur_score=inf; best_a=None
    step=0.3
    stall=0
    last_random=t0
    while time<end:
       if cur_score==inf: p=random; continue
       # occasionally global restart if stall high
       if time - last_random > 20 and stall>200:
          if rng.random()<0.5:
             p = best_p.copy(); p += 0.3*rng.normal(0,1,Dtot); 
          else:
             p = rng.uniform(lower,upper)
             p[0]=rng.uniform(0.5,1.5)
          cur_score, a = _eval_fourier(p,B)
          last_random=time; stall=0; step=0.5
          continue
       idxs=[]
       if rng.random()<0.25:
          idx1=int(rng.integers(0,Dtot)); idxs=[idx1]
       else:
          idx1=int(rng.integers(0,Dtot)); idx2=int(rng.integers(0,Dtot))
          if idx2==idx1: idx2=(idx2+1)%Dtot
          idxs=[idx1,idx2]
       p_new=p.copy()
       for idx in idxs:
          if idx==0:
             p_new[idx]=p_new[idx]*float(np.exp(step*rng.normal(0,1)))
          else:
             p_new[idx]=p_new[idx]+0.25*step*float(rng.normal(0,1))
          p_new[idx]=min(upper[idx],max(lower[idx],p_new[idx]))
       score,a = _eval_fourier(p_new,B)
       if score < cur_score:
          p=p_new; cur_score=score; best_p=p.copy(); best_a=a
          consider(state,pool,a)
          stall=0; step=min(step*1.08, 2.0)
       else:
          step=max(step*0.85,0.02); stall+=1
          if stall>1000: step=0.5; stall=400
```

def _optimize_x_anti(t):
    n=len(t); if n==0: return None, inf
    ones=np.ones(n)
    C=_convolve_fft(t,t)
    D=_convolve_fft(t,ones)
    A=np.arange(1,n)+np.arange(n-1,0,-1)
    xs=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,41)
    best_score=inf; best_x=0; best_a=None
    for x in xs:
       P=A + 2*x*D + x*x*C
       mc=float(np.max(P))
       score=2.0*mc/n
       if score < best_score: ...
    if best_a: return best_a,best_score
    a=np.ones(n)/n; return a, _score_internal(a)
```
This grid not vectorized over x; 41 * n ~ fine. Could vectorize but not needed. To optimize more, if best_x interior, do fine grid around: 81? Use 81. For n=2048, 81*4095 ~332k ops per restart, okay. We'll use 65 and maybe 16 fine around.

Pool top selection without lambda:
```
def _top_pool(pool, k):
    items=list(pool)
    res=[]
    for i in range(len(items)):
        best_idx=i; best_val=items[i][0]
        for j in range(i+1,len(items)):
            if items[j][0] < best_val:
                best_val=items[j][0]; best_idx=j
        # swap i and best_idx
        tmp=items[i]; items[i]=items[best_idx]; items[best_idx]=tmp
        res.append(items[i])
        if len(res)>=k: break
    return res
```

```python
"""
Hybrid search for low self-convolution coefficient sequences.

The program combines:
1. Fast FFT-based scoring of a normalized sequence.
2. A specialized anti-palindromic binary half-sequence construction.
3. Low-dimensional Fourier parameterization searches.
4. Miror-descent-like continuous refinement on the simplex.
5. Random block/crossover perturbations to escape local minima.

All returned sequences are non-negative and normalized to sum 1, which is
scale-invariant for the objective.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def _fast_len(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _convolve_fft(x, y):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0 or y.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = x.size + y.size - 1
    L = _fast_len(out_len)
    fx = np.fft.rfft(x, L)
    fy = np.fft.rfft(y, L)
    z = np.fft.irfft(fx * fy, L)
    return z[:out_len]


def _conv_self(x):
    return _convolve_fft(x, x)


def _normalize_array(x):
    a = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 1e-12:
        return None
    a = a / s
    return a


def _score_internal(a):
    a = _normalize_array(a)
    if a is None:
        return np.inf
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    c = _conv_self(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc):
        return np.inf
    if mc < 0.0:
        mc = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mc)


def _smooth_weights(c, frac):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64) / c.size
    eps = max(1e-300, frac * maxc)
    z = (c - maxc) / eps
    zm = float(np.max(z))
    w = np.exp(z - zm)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        mask = c >= maxc - 1e-12 * maxc
        cnt = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        if cnt > 0:
            w[mask] = 1.0 / cnt
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmax(c))
            w[idx] = 1.0
        return w
    return w / s


def _grad_weighted(w, a):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    L = _fast_len(3 * n - 2)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    fa_rev = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], L)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fw * fa_rev, L)
    return conv[n - 1:n - 1 + n]


def _block_perturb(a, rng, num_blocks=3, sigma=0.25):
    out = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = out.size
    if n == 0:
        return out
    nblocks = max(1, int(num_blocks))
    for _ in range(nblocks):
        max_w = max(2, n // 8)
        w = int(rng.integers(1, max_w + 1))
        if w >= n:
            w = n
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
        out[start:start + w] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return out


def _seed_uniform(n):
    return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)


def _seed_dirichlet(n, alpha, rng):
    return rng.dirichlet(alpha, size=n)


def _seed_blocks(n, r, rng):
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    r = max(1, int(r))
    for _ in range(r):
        max_w = max(2, n // 2)
        w = int(rng.integers(max(1, n // 40), max_w + 1))
        if w >= n:
            w = n
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
        out[start:start + w] += 1.0 / (float(r) * float(w))
    return out


def _resize_sequence(x, n):
    arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return None
    if arr.size == n:
        s = float(np.sum(arr))
        if s <= 0.0:
            return None
        return arr / s
    if arr.size < n:
        old = np.arange(arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        new = np.linspace(0.0, float(arr.size - 1), n)
        out = np.interp(new, old, arr)
    else:
        out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        idx = np.arange(arr.size)
        chunks = np.array_split(idx, n)
        for i, ch in enumerate(chunks):
            out[i] = float(np.mean(arr[ch]))
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(out))
    if s <= 0.0:
        out = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out /= s
    return out


def _consider(state, pool, seq):
    a = _normalize_array(seq)
    if a is None:
        return
    score = _score_internal(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    if score < state["score"] - 1e-12:
        state["seq"] = a.copy()
        state["score"] = float(score)
        elapsed = float(time.monotonic() - state["t0"])
        print(f"new bound (internal) {score:.10f} len={a.size} time={elapsed:.1f}s")
    should_pool = False
    if state["score"] == np.inf:
        should_pool = True
    elif score <= state["score"] + 0.05:
        should_pool = True
    elif len(pool) < 12:
        should_pool = True
    if should_pool and pool is not None:
        pool.append((float(score), a.copy()))
        if len(pool) > 24:
            worst_idx = 0
            worst_val = pool[0][0]
            for i in range(1, len(pool)):
                if pool[i][0] > worst_val:
                    worst_val = pool[i][0]
                    worst_idx = i
            del pool[worst_idx]


def _top_pool(pool, k):
    if not pool:
        return []
    items = list(pool)
    n = len(items)
    out = []
    for i in range(min(k, n)):
        best_idx = i
        best_val = items[i][0]
        for j in range(i + 1, n):
            if items[j][0] < best_val:
                best_val = items[j][0]
                best_idx = j
        tmp = items[i]
        items[i] = items[best_idx]
        items[best_idx] = tmp
        out.append(items[i])
    return out


def _continuous_local_search(a0, budget_s, rng):
    a = _normalize_array(a0)
    if a is None:
        return None
    budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return a
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a

    a = a.astype(np.float64, copy=True)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-10)
    a = a / float(np.sum(a))

    t0 = float(time.monotonic())
    deadline = t0 + budget_s

    cur = _score_internal(a)
    if not np.isfinite(cur):
        return None
    best = a.copy()
    best_score = float(cur)

    step = 0.08
    stall = 0
    eps_phase = 0
    it = 0

    while float(time.monotonic()) < deadline and stall < 12000 and step > 0.003:
        it += 1
        c = _conv_self(a)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        maxc = float(np.max(c))
        if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0:
            break

        frac_list = (0.25, 0.12, 0.05, 0.20, 0.08)
        frac = frac_list[eps_phase % len(frac_list)]
        if stall > 1200:
            frac = 0.30

        w = _smooth_weights(c, frac)
        g = _grad_weighted(w, a)
        if g.size != a.size:
            break
        g = g - float(np.mean(g))
        sd = float(np.std(g))
        if sd < 1e-14 or not np.isfinite(sd):
            a = a + (1.0 / float(n)) * (0.05 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=n))
            a = _normalize_array(a)
            if a is None:
                break
            cur = _score_internal(a)
            if not np.isfinite(cur):
                break
            continue

        u = g / sd
        accepted = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25):
            a_new = a * np.exp(-step * mult * u)
            a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-300)
            sn = float(np.sum(a_new))
            if sn <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sn):
                continue
            a_new = a_new / sn
            snew = _score_internal(a_new)
            if np.isfinite(snew) and snew < cur:
                a = a_new
                cur = float(snew)
                best = a.copy()
                best_score = cur
                accepted = True
                break

        if accepted:
            stall = 0
            step = min(step * 1.07, 0.35)
            if stall == 0 and it % 500 == 0:
                eps_phase += 1
        else:
            step = max(step * 0.78, 0.004)
            stall += 1
            if stall > 250:
                a = _block_perturb(a, rng, 4, 0.30)
                a = _normalize_array(a)
                if a is None:
                    break
                cur = _score_internal(a)
                if not np.isfinite(cur):
                    break
                if cur < best_score:
                    best = a.copy()
                    best_score = float(cur)
                step = 0.10
                stall = 0
                eps_phase += 1

        if it % 800 == 0 and float(np.min(a)) < 1e-9:
            a = a + 1e-9
            a = a / float(np.sum(a))

    return best


def _fourier_eval(p, basis):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size != basis.shape[1] or not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return np.inf, None
    raw = basis @ p
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(raw)):
        return np.inf, None
    a = np.clip(raw, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf, None
    a = a / s
    score = _score_internal(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return np.inf, None
    return score, a


def _fourier_one(n, budget_s, rng, state, pool):
    budget_s = max(0.0, float(budget_s))
    if budget_s <= 0.0:
        return
    n = max(64, int(n))
    t0 = float(time.monotonic())
    deadline = t0 + budget_s

    D = 10
    freqs = list(range(1, D // 2 + 1))
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    cols = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)]
    for f in freqs:
        ang = (2.0 * np.pi * float(f) / float(n)) * i
        cols.append(np.sin(ang))
        cols.append(np.cos(ang))
    basis = np.column_stack(cols)
    Dtot = basis.shape[1]

    lower = np.full(Dtot, -1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    lower[0] = 0.05
    upper = np.full(Dtot, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    upper[0] = 3.0

    def _clip_params(v):
        return np.clip(v, lower, upper)

    p = np.zeros(Dtot, dtype=np.float64)
    p[0] = 1.0
    cur, best_a = _fourier_eval(p, basis)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = cur

    step = 0.25
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_random = t0

    while float(time.monotonic()) < deadline:
        it += 1
        if best_score == np.inf:
            p = np.zeros(Dtot, dtype=np.float64)
            p[0] = 1.0
            cur, best_a = _fourier_eval(p, basis)
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_score = cur
            continue

        if float(time.monotonic()) - last_random > 25.0 and stall > 350:
            last_random = float(time.monotonic())
            if rng.random() < 0.55:
                p = best_p.copy()
                p = _clip_params(p + 0.35 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=Dtot))
            else:
                p = np.zeros(Dtot, dtype=np.float64)
                p[0] = float(rng.uniform(0.4, 1.7))
                p[1:] = 0.5 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=Dtot - 1)
                p = _clip_params(p)
            cur, a = _fourier_eval(p, basis)
            stall = 0
            step = 0.45
            if np.isfinite(cur) and cur < best_score:
                best_score = float(cur)
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_a = a
                _consider(state, pool, best_a)
            continue

        if rng.random() < 0.22:
            idx1 = int(rng.integers(0, Dtot))
            idxs = [idx1]
        else:
            idx = it % Dtot
            idx1 = idx
            idx2 = int(rng.integers(0, Dtot))
            if idx2 == idx1:
                idx2 = (idx2 + 1) % Dtot
            idxs = [idx1, idx2]

        p_new = p.copy()
        for idx in idxs:
            if idx == 0:
                p_new[idx] = p_new[idx] * float(np.exp(step * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)))
            else:
                p_new[idx] = p_new[idx] + 0.28 * step * float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0))
            p_new[idx] = float(np.clip(p_new[idx], lower[idx], upper[idx]))

        score, a = _fourier_eval(p_new, basis)
        if np.isfinite(score) and score < cur:
            p = p_new
            cur = float(score)
            stall = 0
            step = min(step * 1.07, 2.2)
            if cur < best_score:
                best_score = cur
                best_p = p.copy()
                best_a = a
                _consider(state, pool, best_a)
        else:
            stall += 1
            step = max(step * 0.87, 0.02)
            if stall > 900:
                step = 0.60
                stall = 300

    if best_a is not None:
        _consider(state, pool, best_a)


def _random_paly_u(m, rng, mode):
    base_len = (m + 1) // 2
    if mode == 1:
        v = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        nf = int(rng.integers(1, 4))
        maxf = max(2, m // 6)
        for _ in range(nf):
            f = int(rng.integers(1, maxf + 1))
            amp = float(rng.normal(1.0, 0.45))
            v += amp * np.cos((2.0 * np.pi * float(f) / float(m)) * np.arange(m, dtype=np.float64))
        u = np.sign(v)
        u[u == 0.0] = 1.0
        return u.astype(np.float64)
    base = rng.choice([-1.0, 1.0], size=base_len)
    u = np.empty(m, dtype=np.float64)
    u[:base_len] = base
    tail = base[:m - base_len][::-1]
    u[base_len:] = tail
    return u


def _make_anti_t(n, rng, mode=0):
    if n < 2:
        return np.zeros(max(0, int(n)), dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n -= 1
    m = n // 2
    u = _random_paly_u(m, rng, mode)
    t = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t[:m] = u
    t[m:] = -u[::-1]
    return t


def _initial_P_from_t(t, x):
    n = t.size
    if n == 0:
        return None
    ones = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    C = _convolve_fft(t, t)
    D = _convolve_fft(t, ones)
    A = (np.arange(1, n, dtype=np.float64) + np.arange(n - 1, 0, -1, dtype=np.float64))
    P = A + (2.0 * x) * D + (x * x) * C
    return P


def _anti_restart(n, x, rng, max_secs):
    n = max(128, int(n))
    if n % 2 != 0:
        n -= 1
    m = n // 2
    base_len = (m + 1) // 1
    base_len = (m + 1) // 2

    t = _make_anti_t(n, rng, mode=int(rng.integers(0, 3)))
    if t.size != n:
        return None, np.inf

    x = float(np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0))
    P = _initial_P_from_t(t, x)
    if P is None:
        return None, np.inf

    cur = float(np.max(P))
    if not np.isfinite(cur):
        return None, np.inf

    best_t = t.copy()
    best_cur = cur

    T = max(0.5, n / 250.0)
    stall = 0
    t0 = float(time.monotonic())
    deadline = t0 + max(0.1, float(max_secs))

    while float(time.monotonic()) < deadline and stall < 7000:
        idx = int(rng.integers(0, base_len))
        i = idx
        j = m - 1 - idx

        candidates = (i, j, m + i, m + j)
        pos_list = []
        for pp in candidates:
            if 0 <= pp < n and pp not in pos_list:
                pos_list.append(pp)

        if not pos_list:
            break

        P_old = P.copy()
        t_old = [float(t[p]) for p in pos_list]

        x2 = x * x
        for pp in pos_list:
            old_t = float(t[pp])
            delta = -2.0 * old_t
            if delta == 0.0:
                continue
            P[pp:pp + n] += (x * delta) + (2.0 * x2 * delta) * t
            P[2 * pp] += x2 * delta * delta
            t[pp] = old_t + delta

        new_cur = float(np.max(P))
        if not np.isfinite(new_cur):
            P[:] = P_old
            for pp, vv in zip(pos_list, t_old):
                t[pp] = vv
            stall += 1000
            continue

        accept = False
        if new_cur < cur:
            accept = True
            stall = 0
            T = max(0.05, T * 0.92)
            if new_cur < best_cur:
                best_cur = float(new_cur)
                best_t = t.copy()
        else:
            if T > 0.0:
                ratio = (cur - new_cur) / T
                if ratio > -60.0 and rng.random() < float(np.exp(ratio)):
                    accept = True
            if not accept:
                P[:] = P_old
                for pp, vv in zip(pos_list, t_old):
                    t[pp] = vv
                stall += 1
                T = min(T * 1.035, max(1.0, n / 12.0))
            else:
                stall += 1
                T = min(T * 1.01, max(1.0, n / 12.0))

        if stall > 5000:
            break

    return best_t, best_cur


def _optimize_x_anti(t):
    t = np.asarray(t, dtype=np.float64)
    n = t.size
    if n == 0:
        return None, np.inf
    ones = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    C = _convolve_fft(t, t)
    D = _convolve_fft(t, ones)
    A = (np.arange(1, n, dtype=np.float64) + np.arange(n - 1, 0, -1, dtype=np.float64))

    best_score = np.inf
    best_x = 0.0

    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 57)
    for xv in xs:
        xv = float(xv)
        P = A + (2.0 * xv) * D + (xv * xv) * C
        mc = float(np.max(P))
        sc = (2.0 * mc) / float(n)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_x = xv

    lo = max(0.0, best_x - 0.04)
    hi = min(1.0, best_x + 0.04)
    xs2 = np.linspace(lo, hi, 25)
    for xv in xs2:
        xv = float(xv)
        P = A + (2.0 * xv) * D + (xv * xv) * C
        mc = float(np.max(P))
        sc = (2.0 * mc) / float(n)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_x = xv

    best_x = float(np.clip(best_x, 0.0, 1.0))
    if best_x <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    else:
        a = (1.0 + best_x * t) / float(n)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        if s <= 0.0:
            a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        else:
            a = a / s
    return a, best_score


def _anti_family_search(lengths, deadline, rng, state, pool):
    lengths = [int(n) for n in lengths if int(n) % 2 == 0 and 256 <= int(n) <= 8192]
    if not lengths:
        return
    runs = []
    for n in lengths[:3]:
        for xv in (0.55, 0.85):
            runs.append((n, xv))
    if not runs:
        return

    t0 = float(time.monotonic())
    deadline = max(t0, float(deadline))
    share = max(5.0, (deadline - t0) / float(len(runs)))

    for n, xv in runs:
        local_end = min(deadline, float(time.monotonic()) + share)
        while float(time.monotonic()) < local_end and float(time.monotonic()) < deadline:
            remaining = local_end - float(time.monotonic())
            if remaining < 2.0:
                break
            restart_secs = min(18.0, remaining)
            t, _ = _anti_restart(n, xv, rng, restart_secs)
            if t is None or t.size != n:
                continue
            a, sc = _optimize_x_anti(t)
            if a is not None:
                _consider(state, pool, a)
            if float(time.monotonic()) >= deadline:
                break


def _run_seed_phase(state, pool, rng):
    t0 = state["t0"]
    deadline = min(t0 + 25.0, state["end"])

    prev = None
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
            prev = globals()["height_sequence_1"]
    except Exception:
        prev = None

    prev_arr = None
    n_prev = None
    if prev is not None:
        prev_arr = np.asarray(prev, dtype=np.float64)
        if prev_arr.ndim != 1:
            prev_arr = None
        if prev_arr is not None and prev_arr.size > 0:
            n_prev = int(prev_arr.size)

    lengths = []
    if n_prev is not None and 256 <= n_prev <= 8192:
        lengths.append(n_prev)
    for n in (512, 1024, 2048, 4096):
        if n not in lengths:
            lengths.append(n)
    lengths = lengths[:5]

    for n in lengths:
        if float(time.monotonic()) >= deadline:
            break
        n = max(64, int(n))
        _consider(state, pool, _seed_uniform(n))
        _consider(state, pool, _seed_dirichlet(n, 1.0, rng))
        _consider(state, pool, _seed_dirichlet(n, 0.25, rng))
        for r in (3, 5, 8):
            _consider(state, pool, _seed_blocks(n, r, rng))
        if prev_arr is not None:
            res = _resize_sequence(prev_arr, n)
            if res is not None:
                _consider(state, pool, res)


def _run_continuous_pool(pool, deadline, rng, state):
    top = _top_pool(pool, 4)
    if not top:
        return
    t0 = float(time.monotonic())
    deadline = max(t0, float(deadline))
    per = max(1.0, (deadline - t0) / float(len(top)))
    for sc, seq in top:
        remaining = max(0.0, deadline - float(time.monotonic()))
        budget = min(1200.0, remaining, per)
        if budget <= 0.5:
            break
        a = _continuous_local_search(seq, budget, rng)
        if a is not None:
            _consider(state, pool, a)
        if float(time.monotonic()) >= deadline:
            break


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    t0 = float(time.monotonic())

    try:
        total_budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 20.0)
    except Exception:
        total_budget = 2380.0

    end = t0 + total_budget

    state = {
        "t0": t0,
        "end": end,
        "seq": None,
        "score": np.inf
    }
    pool = []

    print(f"objective search starting, budget={total_budget:.1f}s")

    _run_seed_phase(state, pool, rng)

    anti_deadline = min(end, t0 + min(520.0, 0.25 * total_budget + 80.0))
    anti_lengths = []
    if state["seq"] is not None:
        anti_lengths.append(state["seq"].size)
    for n in (1024, 2048, 4096):
        if n not in anti_lengths:
            anti_lengths.append(n)
    _anti_family_search(anti_lengths, anti_deadline, rng, state, pool)

    fourier_deadline = min(end, t0 + min(1400.0, 0.60 * total_budget + 120.0))
    fourier_lengths = []
    for n in (1024, 2048, 4096):
        if n not in fourier_lengths:
            fourier_lengths.append(n)
    f_share = max(30.0, (fourier_deadline - float(time.monotonic())) / float(len(fourier_lengths)))
    for n in fourier_lengths:
        if float(time.monotonic()) >= fourier_deadline:
            break
        _fourier_one(n, min(900.0, f_share, fourier_deadline - float(time.monotonic())), rng, state, pool)

    continuous_deadline = max(float(time.monotonic()) + 10.0, end - 35.0)
    _run_continuous_pool(pool, continuous_deadline, rng, state)

    if state["seq"] is None:
        state["seq"] = _seed_uniform(1024)
        state["score"] = _score_internal(state["seq"])

    seq = np.asarray(state["seq"], dtype=np.float64)
    seq = np.clip(seq, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if seq.size == 0:
        seq = _seed_uniform(1024)

    try:
        exact = float(evaluate_sequence(seq.tolist()))
        if np.isfinite(exact):
            if exact < state["score"]:
                state["score"] = exact
            print(f"exact bound: {exact:.10f} len={seq.size}")
        else:
            print("exact eval invalid, using internal best")
    except Exception:
        print("exact eval unavailable, using internal best")

    out = []
    for vv in seq:
        if not np.isfinite(vv):
            vv = 0.0
        if vv < 0.0:
            vv = 0.0
        if vv > 1000.0:
            vv = 1000.0
        out.append(float(vv))

    if not out:
        out = [1.0]

    print(f"final returned len={len(out)} bound={state['score']:.10f}")
    return out
```
